        Idea:
Refactor pinned shot groups in the video editor to be first-class timeline entities, eliminating an entire class of "shot group disconnects" bugs that come from fragile parallel cohesion logic spread across drag, resize, sync, and render code paths.

## Why

The video editor's pinned shot groups (the colored bordered boxes that visually group N timeline clips under a single shot label) are currently a "soft" abstraction layered on top of flat `row.actions` arrays. The shot group is just a `{ shotId, trackId, clipIds: string[] }` record stored in `data.config.pinnedShotGroups`. Cohesion (the property that all the group's clips stay contiguous on the timeline) is enforced by SEPARATE bespoke logic in every code path that mutates clip positions:

- Drag: `useClipDrag` does "deferred expansion" — when the user grabs one clip in a group, it pulls the group's other clips into the drag set. Then `multi-drag-utils.applyMultiDragMoves` calls `resolveGroupOverlaps` which treats moved clips as one shifted group.
- Resize: `TimelineCanvas` resize handlers compute a `getShotGroupAdjacent` neighbor and write a paired `ResizeOverride` so the immediate neighbor follows the dragged edge.
- Sync: `usePinnedGroupSync` runs on a debounce after the underlying Shot's `images[]` changes; it rebuilds the group's clips one-by-one and shifts subsequent clips on the row.
- Track move / cross-track drag: updates `pinnedShotGroups[].trackId` via `updatePinnedShotGroupTrackIds`.
- Delete / unpin / "deconstruct" / "delete shot": each touches `pinnedShotGroups` differently.

There's no shared "the group is the unit of editing" primitive. Each path reinvents cohesion. Bugs keep emerging in different paths with the same user-visible symptom: "the shot disconnects."

On top of that, the visual contiguity check in `TimelineCanvas.tsx` (`positionedShotGroups`, around lines 446-499) iterates `group.clipIds` IN ARRAY ORDER and checks `current.start < previous.start || current.start - previous.end > snapThreshold`. **This assumes `group.clipIds` is in left-to-right spatial order, but nothing actively maintains that invariant.** `pinGroup` stores `[...selectedClipIds]` (Set iteration order, i.e. click order), and `usePinnedGroupSync` rebuilds in `desiredImages` order from the database. Once array order disagrees with spatial order by even one position, the group's border vanishes — user reports "disconnected."

We just shipped two patches for this in the drag and resize paths. The patches work for their cases but they don't address the root cause: the shot group is not a real entity in the editing model.

## Goal

Make a pinned shot group a FIRST-CLASS timeline entity. Conceptually:

- The shot group has its own bounds (`start`, `end`) on the row.
- It contains child clips with their own positions inside that bound (or relative offsets).
- Drag, resize, selection, and serialization all see the group as ONE thing by default.
- The contiguity invariant is enforced BY CONSTRUCTION, not by validation. Children of a group can never become non-contiguous because they live inside a single owning entity that lays them out.
- Operations on individual clips inside a group are explicit, intentional, and rare (the per-clip context menu is already locked down to asset-state actions only when a clip is in a pinned group, per a recent change).

## Hard requirements

1. **Eliminate the "shot disconnects" bug class.** After this refactor, no drag, resize, sync, or other normal interaction can make a pinned shot group's children become non-contiguous. The only way to break a group is the explicit "Deconstruct shot" or "Delete shot" actions on the shot label menu.

2. **No regression in user-facing behavior** for the supported flows:
   - Pinning a group from a selection.
   - Dragging a group on its row (snaps to bounds, snaps near siblings, can hit timeline start at t=0).
   - Dragging a group cross-track (entire group moves to the new row).
   - Resizing a group's outer left/right edge (stretches/shrinks the group, content reflows).
   - Resizing an interior boundary inside a group (between child clips) — re-evaluate whether this is even meaningful in the new model. If kept, must preserve cohesion.
   - The shot label menu actions (Deconstruct, Delete, Switch to Final Video, Switch to Images, Update to Latest Video, Generate Video, Jump to Shot).
   - Right-clicking a clip inside a pinned group still shows only asset-state actions per the recent change.
   - The `usePinnedGroupSync` debounce still keeps the group's children in sync with the underlying Shot's `images[]`.
   - Final video and image modes still work.
   - Auto-shift on resize from the recent megaplan still works (or is replaced by a better mechanism in the new model).

3. **Persistence stays compatible.** The on-disk timeline config schema currently stores `pinnedShotGroups: { shotId, trackId, clipIds, mode, imageClipSnapshot? }[]`. Decide whether the new model can keep this on-disk shape (with internal restructuring), or whether a schema migration is needed. If a migration is needed, it must be backward-compatible (old configs load correctly).

4. **Remotion preview rendering stays correct.** The Remotion compositions render flat clips. The refactor must not break what Remotion sees — i.e., individual clips inside a group must still be addressable for rendering, even if the editing model treats them as children of a group.

5. **Tests pass.** All existing tests in `src/tools/video-editor/` must continue to pass, OR be updated with clear justification when behavior intentionally changes. New tests should cover the cohesion-by-construction property.

## Design questions the plan must answer

The planning phase needs to investigate the codebase and propose answers to all of these. Do not guess — read the code:

1. **Data model.** Which of these is the right abstraction?
   a) "Group action" embedded in `row.actions`: the row's actions array becomes a heterogeneous list of `TimelineAction | GroupAction`, where `GroupAction` has its own start/end and a `children[]` of TimelineActions. Drag/resize see the GroupAction as a single thing.
   b) "Group resolver" overlay: keep `row.actions` as flat TimelineActions, but introduce a `GroupResolver` that, given a clipId or set of clipIds, returns the group bounds and member list. Drag/resize hooks consult the resolver and operate on the whole group when any member is touched.
   c) Something else.
   Trade off: (a) is conceptually cleaner but ripples through many types and Remotion assumptions; (b) is less invasive but still leaves cohesion as a soft property enforced by callers.

2. **Selection model.** When the user clicks one clip inside a group, do they select (i) the clip, (ii) the group, or (iii) is there a hover/click distinction (e.g., one click = group, double click = clip)? The current behavior is "click selects the clip"; the recent context-menu change limits what that clip can do. Pick a model that makes "you cannot accidentally break a group by editing one of its clips" obvious.

3. **Internal layout.** Inside a group, children are positioned. Are positions stored absolutely (each child has its own row-relative start/end) or relatively (each child has an offset from the group's start)? Relative positioning makes "shift the whole group by N seconds" trivial and impossible to break; absolute positioning means children can drift relative to each other (the original bug). Strongly prefer relative.

4. **Interior boundaries.** Today, a user can resize the boundary between two child clips of a group via the auto-shift mechanism we added. Decide whether interior boundary resize is still allowed in the new model. If yes, define it precisely. If no, document why not (likely it should not be — the user already said they don't really want per-clip operations inside a group).

5. **Sync interplay.** `usePinnedGroupSync` watches the Shot's `images[]` and rewrites the group's clips. In the new model, this becomes "rewrite the children of the group in place." Specify how this interacts with in-progress drags/resizes (it currently debounces; does that still work?).

6. **Migration / persistence.** Define the on-disk shape and the load-time conversion if needed. Old configs must load.

7. **Snap & timeline-start clamp.** Today the bug-fix from a few minutes ago lets `findBestGroupStart` clamp the group to t≥0 as a unit. In the new model, the group's `start` would be clamped directly. Make sure that still happens in every drag/resize path.

8. **Reuse of existing infrastructure.** `multi-drag-utils.ts`, `resolve-overlaps.ts`, `useClipDrag.ts`, `useClipResize.ts`, `usePinnedShotGroups.ts`, and `useShotGroups.ts` all encode pieces of cohesion. Decide which become obsolete and which get repurposed. Avoid leaving dead code.

## Files most likely to change

Start by reading these to ground the plan. Many more files will surface — explore from these as anchors:

- Data shape and config:
  - src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (look for PinnedShotGroup, TimelineConfig)
  - src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts (resolve config -> TimelineData)
- Group accessors and sync:
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useShotGroups.ts
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts (pinGroup, unpinGroup, updatePinnedGroup, usePinnedGroupSync)
- Drag:
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts (expandPinnedGroupDrag, deferredPinnedGroupClipIds)
  - src/tools/video-editor/lib/multi-drag-utils.ts (planMultiDragMoves, applyMultiDragMoves, updatePinnedShotGroupTrackIds)
  - src/tools/video-editor/lib/resolve-overlaps.ts (resolveGroupOverlaps, findBestGroupStart)
  - src/tools/video-editor/lib/snap-edges.ts
- Resize:
  - src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx (resize handlers, getShotGroupAdjacent, positionedShotGroups)
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipResize.ts (auto-shift transaction logic from the recent megaplan)
- Render and editor wiring:
  - src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx
  - src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.tsx
  - src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ShotGroupContextMenu.tsx
- Provider / state:
  - src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.tsx
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.types.ts
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.contexts.ts
- Final video / image mode switching:
  - src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts (or similarly named)
- Remotion / preview:
  - Whatever in src/tools/video-editor/compositions/ and src/tools/video-editor/components/PreviewPanel/ reads the timeline config -- verify it does not depend on flat row.actions in a way the new model breaks.

## Constraints

- Scope is the video-editor tool only. No DB schema changes, no edge functions, no migrations to Supabase. (The on-disk config is a JSON blob in the user's project file.)
- Follow the rules in CLAUDE.md at the repo root and in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/CLAUDE.md.
- Prefer Edit over Write. No new docs unless explicitly necessary.
- Don't introduce new state management libraries or routing.
- Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Keep the diff focused on the shot-group refactor.
- Pre-existing failing tests (one in TimelineCanvas.test.tsx about data-shot-group-kind="pinned") are unrelated and should be left alone.
- Reuse existing UI primitives in src/shared/components/ui/. No new color tokens.

## What success looks like

1. After the refactor, manually attempting to break a pinned shot group via any combination of normal drag, resize, snap, multi-select, or cross-track drag is impossible. The only way to break a group is the explicit Deconstruct/Delete actions.
2. Type check passes (npx tsc --noEmit).
3. Existing video-editor tests pass, plus new tests covering the cohesion-by-construction property.
4. The Remotion preview still renders the same output for an unchanged timeline.
5. The diff is concentrated in the video-editor tool with a clear before/after for the new abstraction.

        Plan:

# Implementation Plan: Pinned Shot Groups as First-Class Timeline Entities (rev 3)

## Overview

Pinned shot groups are a sidecar `{ shotId, trackId, clipIds[], mode, imageClipSnapshot? }` record on `TimelineConfig`. Cohesion is enforced ad-hoc by drag deferred-expansion, resize paired-overrides, sync rebuild, and a contiguity check that wrongly assumes `clipIds` is in spatial order. The disconnect bug class follows.

**Approach (settled):** Hybrid — group-centric authoring with `start: number` + `children: { clipId, offset, duration }[]` (relative offsets), flat `TimelineConfig.clips[]` retained as the Remotion source of truth, projected from `group.start + child.offset` via a single `projectPinnedShotGroups(config)` normalizer. Cohesion-by-construction.

**Rev 3 fixes the three concrete root causes left from rev 2:**

1. **Keyboard delete/split on a group member is a no-op** (FLAG-002, issue_hints-1, callers-2). Rev 2 wrongly proposed rerouting to delete-shot; the brief is explicit that ONLY the shot-label-menu Delete/Deconstruct may break a group. ArrowUp/ArrowDown and `moveSelectedClipsToTrack` still reroute to whole-group moves because moving the group does not break cohesion.

2. **`useExternalDrop` builds the group inside its own loop** (FLAG-003, issue_hints-2, correctness-2, callers-1, all_locations-2, scope-3, scope, issue_hints-3) — using the `timeOffset` accumulator and `nextEdit.duration` it already tracks at `useExternalDrop.ts:148-169`. The new helper takes a `children` list directly; no need to read `workingData.config.clips`. The selection-pinning caller uses a thin convenience wrapper that derives children from `config.clips`.

3. **`isInteractionActive()` is a shared ref lifted into the editor state layer**, not on `useDragCoordinator` alone (correctness-1, all_locations-1). Both drag (in `useClipDrag`) and resize (in `TimelineCanvas`'s `resizeSessionRef`/`pendingOpsRef` lifecycle) write to it; `usePinnedGroupSync` reads it.

The hybrid model, projection wiring, clone helpers, Delete-shot semantics preservation, and broader edit surface from rev 2 are all preserved.

Scope: video-editor tool only.

---

## Phase 1: Foundation — Model, Projection, Clone Helpers, Migration

### Step 1: Extend the PinnedShotGroup type (`src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Edit** `PinnedShotGroup` (`src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts:169`) to add `start: number` and `children: { clipId: string; offset: number; duration: number }[]`. Keep `clipIds` (derived; JSDoc warning), `mode`, `videoAssetKey`, `imageClipSnapshot`.
2. **Document the invariant** in a comment: `children` is spatially ordered; `clipIds === children.map(c => c.clipId)`; `children[i].offset + children[i].duration === children[i+1].offset`; `start >= 0`.

### Step 2: Add projection + group operations module (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/pinned-group-projection.ts` — new)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/lib/pinned-group-projection.ts`. Pure functions:
   - `projectPinnedShotGroups(config: TimelineConfig): TimelineConfig` — for each group, rewrites every member clip in `config.clips[]` with `start = group.start + child.offset`, `end = start + child.duration`, `track = group.trackId`.
   - **`buildGroupFromChildren(args: { shotId, trackId, start, children, mode, extras? }): PinnedShotGroup`** — primitive constructor. Caller supplies the `children` list directly (already in spatial order). Used by `useExternalDrop` (which has child geometry from its own loop) and by the next helper.
   - `buildGroupFromConfigClips(args: { shotId, trackId, clipIds, config, mode, extras? }): PinnedShotGroup` — convenience wrapper. Looks up each clipId in `config.clips[]`, sorts by `clip.start`, derives `start = max(0, min(clip.start))`, and calls `buildGroupFromChildren`. Used by `pinGroup` (selection pinning).
   - `moveGroup(config, groupKey, nextStart, nextTrackId?)` — clamps `nextStart >= 0`; updates `group.start`/`trackId`.
   - `resizeGroupEdge(config, groupKey, edge: 'left'|'right', deltaSeconds)` — uniform-scale child durations; recomputes contiguous offsets; left edge also shifts `group.start`.
   - `replaceGroupChildren(config, groupKey, nextChildren)` — preserves `group.start`.
   - `normalizeChildren(children)` — sort by offset, butt-collapse, renumber.
   - `findEnclosingPinnedGroup(config, clipId): PinnedShotGroup | null`.
   - `categorizeSelection(clipIds, config) → { freeClips, affectedGroups }` — used by Step 12.
2. Every group writer ends by calling `normalizeChildren`. Projection runs at the commit boundary (Step 3).

### Step 3: Wire projection into `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry` AND `applyEdit` commit boundary (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-save-utils.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Edit** `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry` (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-save-utils.ts:23–51`): immediately after `repairConfig(migrateToFlatTracks(config))`, call `migratedConfig = projectPinnedShotGroups(migratedConfig)` BEFORE `resolvedConfig` is constructed. Do NOT project inside `assembleTimelineData`.
2. **Edit** `useTimelineCommit.ts` `applyEdit` for type `'config'` and type `'pinnedShotGroups'`: on entry, run the incoming config/override through `projectPinnedShotGroups` so callers passing whole `resolvedConfig` objects (`useClipEditing.ts:337–386`, `useTimelineTrackManagement.ts:99–127`) cannot bypass projection. Type `'rows'` projection still flows through `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry`.
3. **Verify** by grep that no other materialization path builds rows from raw `config`.

### Step 4: Update every clone helper (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/migrate.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Edit** `clonePinnedShotGroups` in `src/tools/video-editor/lib/migrate.ts:163–172` to deep-copy `start` and `children`:
   ```ts
   pinnedShotGroups?.map((group) => ({
     ...group,
     clipIds: [...group.clipIds],
     children: group.children?.map((c) => ({ ...c })),
     imageClipSnapshot: group.imageClipSnapshot?.map((s) => ({ ...s, meta: { ...s.meta } })),
   }));
   ```
2. **Edit** the equivalent helper in `src/tools/video-editor/lib/timeline-data.ts:91–100` and the per-mutation clone in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts:31–39` the same way.
3. **Audit** with `Grep` for ad-hoc `PinnedShotGroup` clones inside `src/tools/video-editor/`.

### Step 5: Load-time migration for legacy configs (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/migrate.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add** an idempotent upgrader called from `repairConfig`: for any `PinnedShotGroup` missing `start` or `children`, look up each `clipId` in `config.clips[]`, drop missing ids, sort by `clip.start`, set `start = max(0, min(clip.start))`, build `children` with offsets relative to that start. Existing `start`+`children` → no-op.

### Step 6: Unit tests for projection + group ops + migration (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/pinned-group-projection.test.ts` — new)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add** projection tests, `buildGroupFromChildren`, `buildGroupFromConfigClips`, `moveGroup` clamp, `resizeGroupEdge` uniform scaling, `replaceGroupChildren` preserves start, legacy migration, and a property test asserting cohesion-by-construction after random sequences of group ops.

## Phase 2: Shared Interaction State + Write-Path Refactor

### Step 7: Lift interaction state into the editor state layer (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.contexts.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Add** an `interactionStateRef: MutableRefObject<{ drag: boolean; resize: boolean }>` and an `isInteractionActive()` accessor inside the editor state layer (`useTimelineState.contexts.ts:98–161`). Expose both via the existing editor context. **Reason for lifting:** drag liveness lives in `useClipDrag`, resize liveness lives in `TimelineCanvas` (`resizeSessionRef`/`pendingOpsRef` at `TimelineCanvas.tsx:737–843`); a single shared ref is the only place both can write to without circular hooks. (Settled decision: rev 2 wrongly assumed `useDragCoordinator` already aggregated this.)
2. **Edit** `useClipDrag.ts` start/end paths to set `interactionStateRef.current.drag = true` on pointerdown after threshold and `false` on pointerup/cancel.
3. **Edit** `TimelineCanvas.tsx` `handleResizePointerDown` (`:777–843`) and `endResize` (`:737–775`) to set `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`/`false`. Place the writes adjacent to the existing `pendingOpsRef.current += 1` / `-= 1` so the lifecycle is symmetric.
4. `isInteractionActive()` returns `state.drag || state.resize`. Used by `usePinnedGroupSync` (Step 10).

### Step 8: Drag — single GroupDragEntry (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/multi-drag-utils.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/resolve-overlaps.ts`)
**Scope:** Large

1. **Edit** `useClipDrag.ts` (`:75, 215–237, 278–281`): on pointerdown over a clip in a pinned group, record `GroupDragEntry { groupKey, originStart, originTrackId }`. Delete `expandPinnedGroupDrag` and `deferredPinnedGroupClipIds`.
2. **Edit** `planMultiDragMoves` / `applyMultiDragMoves` (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/multi-drag-utils.ts:248, 302`) to accept heterogeneous moves `{ kind: 'clip', ... } | { kind: 'group', groupKey, nextStart, nextTrackId }`. Group moves call `moveGroup`.
3. **Simplify** `resolveGroupOverlaps` / `findBestGroupStart` (`src/tools/video-editor/lib/resolve-overlaps.ts:81, 216`) to take a caller-provided `{ start, end }` extent. `findBestGroupStart` remains the t≥0 clamp authority.
4. **Audit** `updatePinnedShotGroupTrackIdsFromClipTrackMap` (`:47–80`) and delete if all callers migrate.

### Step 9: Resize — outer-edge only (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipResize.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Edit** `useClipResize.ts`: if the resized clip is inside a pinned group, reject. Outer-edge resize is initiated from a new group-handle entrypoint.
2. **Edit** `TimelineCanvas.tsx` resize handlers + `getShotGroupAdjacent`: delete the bespoke paired-`ResizeOverride` logic.
3. **Edit** `positionedShotGroups` (`TimelineCanvas.tsx:446–499`): replace the in-array-order contiguity check with a direct read of `group.start` + `last(children).offset + duration`. Delete the contiguity check.
4. **Add** group-edge resize handles to the overlay; wire them to `startGroupEdgeResize(groupKey, edge)` in `useClipResize`.

### Step 10: Sync — `replaceGroupChildren` + interaction-state guard (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Edit** `usePinnedGroupSync` (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts:153–404`): inside the debounced effect callback, read `isInteractionActive()` (from the editor context, see Step 7). If true, skip this tick and re-arm the debounce timer so sync runs after the interaction ends. Apply changes via `replaceGroupChildren(config, groupKey, nextChildren)` where `nextChildren` is built by walking `desiredImages` in order with each image's clip duration. `group.start` preserved.
2. **Edit** `pinGroup` (`:98–112`) to delegate to `buildGroupFromConfigClips`.
3. **Edit** `updatePinnedGroup` (`:121–144`) to never write a raw `clipIds` array.
4. **Edit** `reconcilePinnedShotGroupsInRows` (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineCommit.ts:108–150+`) to operate on `children[]`, preserving `group.start`.

### Step 11: Creation paths — `useExternalDrop` builds group inline from loop state (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Edit** `useExternalDrop.ts:128–193`. The existing loop already accumulates `timeOffset += nextEdit.duration` (`:160`) and pushes each `nextEdit.clipId` into `createdClipIds`. Extend the loop to accumulate a parallel `children` list:
   ```ts
   const children: { clipId: string; offset: number; duration: number }[] = [];
   // inside the for-loop, after `createdClipIds.push(nextEdit.clipId)`:
   children.push({ clipId: nextEdit.clipId, offset: timeOffset, duration: nextEdit.duration });
   // (note: push BEFORE the `timeOffset += nextEdit.duration` line so offset is the running cumulative)
   ```
2. **Replace** the inline group construction at `:176–181` with a call to `buildGroupFromChildren({ shotId: shot.id, trackId: resolvedTarget.trackId, start: Math.max(0, dropPosition.time), children, mode: 'images' })`. This bypasses the rev-2 problem entirely: no need to read `workingData.config.clips`, because the loop already has all the geometry.
3. The downstream `applyEdit({ type: 'rows', ... pinnedShotGroupsOverride: [...existing, newGroup] })` is unchanged; the commit-boundary projection in Step 3 normalizes the output.

### Step 12: Broader edit surface — block clip-level breakage on group members (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipEditing.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useKeyboardShortcuts.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineTrackManagement.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Decision (corrected from rev 2):** the brief says ONLY the explicit shot-label-menu Deconstruct/Delete may break a group. Therefore:
   - **Delete (clip-level, including keyboard Backspace/Delete):** if the targeted clip is a group member, **NO-OP**. Rationale comment in code: "Deleting individual group members would break cohesion. The shot label menu's Delete shot action is the only path that may delete grouped clips." A non-blocking error toast is acceptable per CLAUDE.md (errors only).
   - **Split (clip-level, including keyboard `s`):** if the targeted clip is a group member, **NO-OP** with the same toast.
   - **ArrowUp/ArrowDown / `moveSelectedClipsToTrack`:** if the selection contains group members, reroute to a single whole-group move via `moveGroup(config, groupKey, sameStart, nextTrackId)`. Moving the group does NOT break cohesion, so this is allowed.
2. **Edit** `useClipEditing.ts:220–236` (single-clip delete) and `:351–386` (split): use `findEnclosingPinnedGroup` from Step 2 to detect the group case and short-circuit. Free clips keep the existing path.
3. **Edit** `useKeyboardShortcuts.ts:96–112`: same short-circuit at the call sites (`splitSelectedClip()`, `deleteSelectedClip()`). Cleanest place is to gate inside `useClipEditing`'s implementations so both keyboard and menu callers go through one guard.
4. **Edit** `useTimelineTrackManagement.ts:244–331` `moveSelectedClipsToTrack`: before grouping by row, run `categorizeSelection(clipIds, config)`. For each affected group, plan a single group track-move via `moveGroup`. Free clips keep the per-clip path.
5. **Verify** `useTimelineState.contexts.ts:98–161` exports do not need to change (the redirects are inside the existing hook implementations, not at the context boundary).

### Step 13: Shot-group context-menu actions — preserve current semantics (`src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineEditor.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Verify and preserve** `TimelineEditor.tsx:691–697`:
   - **Unpin / Deconstruct** = `unpinGroup(...)` only. Member clips remain at their last projected positions.
   - **Delete shot** = `handleDeleteClips(group.clipIds)` THEN `unpinGroup(...)`. Both clips and the group entry are removed. **Do not change.**
2. **Edit** `useSwitchToFinalVideo` and the switch-to-images counterpart to go through `replaceGroupChildren` (preserving `group.start`).

## Phase 4: Validation

### Step 17: Update existing tests + add new ones
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Update** for the new shape:

   - `src/tools/video-editor/lib/multi-drag-utils.test.ts` — assert `moveGroup` is called for group entities
   - `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipDrag.test.tsx` — assert single `GroupDragEntry`
   - `src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/ClipAction.test.tsx` — no per-clip handles for group members
   - `src/tools/video-editor/components/TimelineEditor/TimelineCanvas.test.tsx` — `positionedShotGroups` reads `group.start`/`children` (leave the pre-existing failing `data-shot-group-kind="pinned"` case alone)
   - `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/__tests__/resolve-overlaps.test.ts` — caller-provided extent API
   - `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useExternalDrop.test.tsx` — assert dropped shots create groups with `start` + spatially-ordered `children`, built from loop state
   - `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineTrackManagement.test.ts` — assert ArrowUp/ArrowDown / `moveSelectedClipsToTrack` on a group member moves the whole group
   - **New** test cases for `useClipEditing` / `useKeyboardShortcuts`: keyboard Backspace/Delete on a selected group member is a no-op (group still present, clips intact); same for `s` (split). (If existing test files cover delete/split, extend them rather than creating new files.)
2. **Add** `src/tools/video-editor/lib/pinned-group-cohesion.test.ts`: property test (random sequences of group ops → contiguous, spatially-sorted), cross-track group move, negative-`start` clamp.
3. **Add** a commit-boundary integration test: legacy config → `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry` → assert (a) member clip starts in resulting `resolvedConfig.clips` match `group.start + child.offset`, (b) `signature` is computed from the projected config, (c) `applyEdit({ type: 'config', ... })` with a pre-projection config still ends up projected.
4. **Add** a sync-deferral test: while `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`, calling the `usePinnedGroupSync` debounced callback does NOT mutate the group; after `resize = false`, the next tick applies.

### Step 18: Type check + full suite
**Scope:** Small

1. `npx tsc --noEmit`
2. `vitest run src/tools/video-editor/`

### Step 19: Manual smoke (info)
**Scope:** Small

1. Pin → drag (incl. t=0) → drag cross-track → resize outer edges → interior resize blocked → keyboard Delete on a group member is a no-op (group intact, error toast) → keyboard `s` on a group member is a no-op → ArrowUp/Down on a group member moves whole group → drop a shot from the panel (group has `start`+`children`) → trigger sync mid-resize (deferred) → shot label menu actions all work as before → Remotion preview unchanged.

---

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 (model + projection + clone helpers + migration + tests).
2. Step 7 (interaction state ref) before Steps 8/9/10 — they depend on it.
3. Phase 2 in order: Step 7 → 8 (drag) → 9 (resize) → 10 (sync) → 11 (external drop) → 12 (edit surface) → 13 (menu verify).
4. Phase 3 after Phase 2 compiles.
5. Phase 4 tests run continuously; full suite + tsc last.

## Validation Order
1. `pinned-group-projection.test.ts` (Step 6) — fastest invariant check.
2. `pinned-group-cohesion.test.ts` (Step 17) — property test.
3. Updated existing tests, including `useExternalDrop.test.tsx`, `useTimelineTrackManagement.test.ts`, and the new keyboard-no-op cases.
4. Commit-boundary integration test (Step 17.3).
5. Sync-deferral test (Step 17.4).
6. `npx tsc --noEmit`.
7. Full `vitest run src/tools/video-editor/`.
8. Manual smoke (Step 19, info).

        "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-089",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-090",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-105",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the cross-track multi-drag new-track branch in `useclipdrag`. that path still commits through `applyedit({ type: 'config', resolvedconfig })` using `buildconfigfromdragresult()`, and the revised plan does not explain how pinned-group metadata is updated atomically on that path, so grouped drags that create a new track are still under-specified even beyond the stale-`trackid` problem.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "DEBT-036",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised step 6 against `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts:12`, `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/index.ts:6`, `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts:2`, `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-calls.ts:1-4`, and `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/selectedclips.ts:1`. the plan still does not account for `isrecord` being part of `messages.ts`'s exported api today, so the utility extraction is incomplete unless it either re-exports `isrecord` from `messages.ts` or updates all four downstream imports.",

      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-video-and-audio-clip-20260406-0616"

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised dismissal model against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usestalevariants.ts:160-166` and `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:39-60`. the plan still dismisses by `shotid`, not by final-video generation id, so if a shot gets a newer final output later in the same session the badge would remain suppressed even though `useshotfinalvideos()` would surface a different `id`; that earlier concern is still open.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",

      "occurrence_count": 2,

        "id": "DEBT-041",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately.",
        "occurrence_count": 2,
        "plan_ids": [
          "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-055",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-056",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-057",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-070",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-video-and-audio-clip-20260406-0616"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-076",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-077",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-089",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-090",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-105",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the cross-track multi-drag new-track branch in `useclipdrag`. that path still commits through `applyedit({ type: 'config', resolvedconfig })` using `buildconfigfromdragresult()`, and the revised plan does not explain how pinned-group metadata is updated atomically on that path, so grouped drags that create a new track are still under-specified even beyond the stale-`trackid` problem.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
        ]
      }
    ]
